PART II - Section 3 - Sub -section (11) 
( रक्षा मंत्रालय को छोरकर ) भारत सरकार के मंत्रालयों मोर ( संघ राज्य क्षेत्र प्रशासनों को छोड़कर )

Statutory Orders and Notifications issued by the Ministries of the Government of India 

( other than the Ministry of Defence ) by Central Authorities 

(other than the Administrations of Union Territories )

लिए रिटगिन प्राफीमर के रूप ने पदाभिहित करना ।

of the Parliamentary Constitucncy of Andhra Pradesh .

के कृभ्यो के पालन में महायता करने के लिए महायक रिटर्निंग

निर्वाचन पायोग द्वारा सारणी के स्तम्भ 2 मे यथा विनिर्दिष्ट समम 

मरकार के प्रत्येक आफिसर को ऐसे मरकारी आफिसर को संसदीय 
निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के रिटर्निंग प्राफिमर की सहायता करने के लिए नियुक्ति । 
Election Cominission hereby appoints each of the oficers 

of Govt. of Assam as specified in Column 2 of the table 
below to assist the Returning Oficer of the Parliamentary 

Constituency in the State of Assam . 
निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा बिहार सरकार के परामर्श में बिहार राज्य म 

संसदीय निर्वाचन क्षेत्रो के लिए रिटर्निंग प्राफिमर और सहायक 
रिनिंग प्राफिमर को पदाभिहित करना । 
Election Commission designates Returning Officer and Asstt. 

Returning Officers in consultation with the Govt. of Bihar 
in respect of Parliamentary Constituencies in the State of 

Bihar. 
निर्वाचन आयोग गुजरात मरकार के परामर्श से सरकारी प्राफिसरों के 

पामने गजगत राज्य संसदीय निर्वाचन क्षेत्र में रिटर्निंग प्राफिमर 

के रूप ने मदाभिहित । 
Election Commission designates in consultation with the Govt . 
of Gujarat the officer of Govt, as Returning Officer of the 

Parliamentary Constituency in the State of Gujarat. 
निर्वाचन आयोग गुजरात सरकार के प्राफिगरो में से प्रत्येक को ऐसे 

सरकारी प्राफिमर तथा प्राफिमरो के सामने गुजरात राज्य के ममदीय 
निर्वाचन क्षेत्र मे रिनिंग आफिसर की गहायता के लिए नियुधिन । 
Election Commission hereby appoints cach of the officers of 
Govt, as Assistant Retuning Officer to assist the Returning 
Ollicer of the Parliamentary Constituency in the State of 

Gujarat. 
निर्वाचन पायोग हरियाणा राज्य सरकार के पराम में सरकारी प्रापिगर 

को में सरकारी आफिसर के मामन हरियाणा राज्य ममदीय निर्वाचन 
क्षेत्रों के लिए रिटनिंग आफिमर के रूप ने पदाभिहित करना । 
Election Commission hereby designates , in consultation with 
the Govt . of Halyana, the officer of Govt . as the Returning 
Ollicer of the Parliamentary Constituencies in the State of 
Haryana .

निर्वाचन आयोग , गिक्किम मरकार के परामर्श से , जिला मजिस्ट्रेट तथा

चन क्षेत्र के लिये रिटर्निंग प्राफिसर के रूप में पदाभिहित करना है ।

of Sikkim , designates the District Magistrate und District 
Collector , Fast District, Gangtok to be the Returning Officer 

for the Parliamentary Constituency for Sikkim .

प्राफिमरों की मिक्किम के लिये मसदीय निर्वाचन - क्षेत्र के लिये महायक 
रिटर्निंग आफिमर के रूप में नियुक्ति । । 
Election Commission , in consultation with the Government 

of Sikkim , appoints the Officers of Government as Assis 
tant Returning Officers for the Parliamcntary constituency 

for Sikkim . 
निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा , ममिलनाडु सरकार के परामर्श में , सरकारी 

आफिमर को तमिलनाडु राज्य के समदीय निर्वाचन- क्षेत्र के लिये रिटानग 

आफिसर के रूप में पदाभिहित करना । 
Election Commission , in consultation with the Government 

of Tamil Nadu, designates the Officer of Government as the 
Returning Officer of the Parliamentary Constituioncy in 
the State of Tamil Nadu . 
निर्वाचन पायोग द्वारा प्रत्येक सरकारी प्राफिमर की तमिलनाड राज्य 
समदीय निर्वाचन - क्षेत्रो के लिये महायक रिटर्निंग आफिसर के रूप में 

नियुक्ति । 
Election Commission appoints cach of the Officers of Govern 
ment as Assistant Returning Officer of the Parliamentary 

Constituency in the State of Tamil Nadu .

को त्रिपुरा राज्य के संमदीय निर्वाचन- क्षेत्र के लिये रिटनिंग, आफिसर 

के रूप में पदाभिहित करना । 
Elcction Commission designates, in consultation with the Govern 
ment of Tripura , the Officer of Government as the Return 
ing Officer of the Parliamentary Constituency in the State 

of Tripura . 
निर्वाचन भायोग द्वारा निपुग सरकार के प्रत्येक माफिसर की विपरा राज्य 

संसदीय निर्वाचन - क्षेत्र के लिये महायक रिटनिग माफिसर के मप म 

नियुक्ति । 
Election Commission appoints cach of the Officers of Govern 
ment of Tripura as Assistant Returning Officer of the Parlia 

mentary Constituency in the State of Tripura . 
निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा उसर प्रदेश सरकार के परामर्श से , सरकारी 

प्राफिसर को उसर प्रदेश राज्य के संसदीय निर्वाचन- क्षनो के लिये 

रिटनिंग आफिसर के रूप में पदाभिहित करना ।

प्रदेश राज्य में संसदीय निर्वाचन क्षेत्रों के लिये साहयफ रिटनिग आफिसर 

के रूप में नियुक्ति । 
Election Commission appoints Officer or Officers of Govern 
ment us Assistant Returning Officer of the Parliumcntury 

Constituencies in the State of Uttar Pradesh ,

क्षेत्र के बारे में उस संसदीय क्षेत्र में समाविष्ट मभी निर्वाचन 
क्षेत्रो के मभी रिटर्निंग आफिमरो तथा महायक निनिग 
प्राफिमरो को उस समदीय निर्वाचन - क्षेत्र में रिटनिग प्राफिमर की 
महायता करने के लिये नियुक्ति ।

this Collectorate Notification No. 3/67 dated 19- 1-67 will stand 
cancelled with the issue of this notification . The powers dclc 
kated to th : Assistant Collectors of Central Excise under Rule 
145 (b ) as per this Collectorate Notification No. 3 /67 dated 
19-1-67 will stand modified to the extent indicated above .

प्रति को रद्द माना जाए ।

S. O . 2120. - - Whercas the Central Government is 
of opinion that for the conservation and development of minerals 
in India , it is necessary to collect as precise information as possi 
ble with regard to any mineral available in or under the lands 
Specified in the Table below in relation to which mining leases 
have been granted hy the Government of Orissa ; 

Now , therefore , un exercise of the powers conferred by 
Sub -section ( 1 ) of section 18A of the Mines and Minerals (Regu 
lation and Development ) Act, 1957 (67 of 1957 ), the Central 
Government after consultation with the Governinent of Orissa , 
hereby authorises the Geological Survey of India to carry out 
such detailed investigations for the purpose of oltaining such 
information as may be necessary in relation to the lands specie 
fied in the said Table .

S . O . 2125 . - In pursuance of rule 45 of the Fundamental 
Rulcs, the President hereby makes the following rules fur 
ther to amend the Supplementary Rules, issued the Govern 
ment of India , late Finance Department, letter No. 104 
C .S .R ., dated 4th February , 1922 , namely : 

In part VIII of the said rules , for Division XXVI- J and 
the heading und rules thereunder, the following Division , 
hending and rulcs shall be substituted namely : 

" Division XXVI-J - The Indian Agricultural Research Ins 
tituto (Allotment of Residences ) Rules, 1977 – 

S . R . 317 -1- 1 : Short title , application and commence 
ment : 

( 1 ) These rules may be called the Indian Agricultural 
Research Institute ( Allotment of Residences ) Rules, 1977 . 

( 2 ) They shall apply to the residential buildings belong 
ing to thc Government and located within the premises of 
the Indian Agricultural Research Institute , 

( 3 ) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette . 


( 8 ) Government means the Central Government; 

(b ) Institute means the Indian Agricultural Rosearch 
Institute , New Delhi; 

( i ) licence fec means the sum of money payable monthly 
in rccoidince with the provisions of the Fundamental Rules 
in respect of a residenco allotted under those rules; 

W priority date of an officer for llotment of accommo 
dation under these rules means the earliest dato of holding 
continuous qualifying appointment on a post under the IDS 
litute including the periods of authorised absence on depu 
tition , foreign service training, leuve cto : 

Provided that the eligibility for the type of residence under 
The provision of S R. 317 - 1 - 5 shall be determined on the 
emoluments on the first day of the allotment year as may 
he siccifica : 

Provided further the officers eligible to Type V or Type 
VI shall also be eligible to allotment of residences in type 
next below to their eligible type, namely , Tyro IV and Type 
V respectively : 

Provided also that where the priority date of two or more 
officers is the same seniority amongst thomselves shall bo 
detcrinined by the amount of emoluments , the officer in 
receipt of higher emoluments taking procedure over the 
officer in l eceipt of lower emoluments ; and where the emo 
Juments are equal, hy th¢ length of service ; 

( 6 ) residence means any residence for the time being 
under the administrative control of the Director ; 

( 1 ) subletting includes sharing of accommodation by an 
allottee with another person with or without payment of 
licence fcc by such other person . 

Explanation Any sharing of accommodation be an allo 
ttee with close relations shall not be deemed to be sub 
(etting; 

( 111 ) transfer means a transfer from an office of the 
Jostitute at New Delhi to any other office of tho Institute 
outside New Delhi or vice versa and also deputation to a 
post in an ineligible office or organisation ; 

( n ) temporary transfer means a transfer which involves 
un absence for a period of not exceeding four months; 

( 0 ) " Type in relation to an Officer means the type of resi 
dence to which he shall be cligible under S . R . 317 - J - S . 

S. R , 317-5-3 Ineligibility of officers owning houses for 
allotment under these rules : - - 

No officer shall be eligible for allotment of a residence 
under these rules if he /she owns a house in full or in part in 
his /lier own name or in the name of his / her wife or hus 
band or any dependent within the municipal limits of New 
Delhi Delhi, 

S . R . 317 -1 - 4 Allotment to husband and wife. Eligibility 
in cases of officers who are married to each other : 

11 ) Where both husband and wife are employed under the 
Institute , the title of each of the two oficers to allotment 
of a residence under these rules shall be considered inde 
pendently . 


S . R . 317 -J - 2 ; Definitions ; - In these rules, unless the 
context otherwise requires : 

( a ) allotment means the grant uf a licence to occupy 
a residence in accordance with the provisions of these rules ; 

(b ) allotment year means the year beginning on 1st Jan 
uary or guch or other period 29 may be notified by the 
Director. 

( c ) Director means the Director of the Institutc und 
includey any other officer authorised by him to act on his 
behalf; 

( d ) eligiblc office means the office of the Indian Agri 
cultural Research Institute and its sub -stations, the staff of 
which has been declared by the Director as cligible for acco 
mmodation under these rules ; 

( e ) emoluments means the emoluments as defined in 
rule 45 - C of the Fundamental Rules , but excluding the com 
pensatory allowance . 

Explanation - In the case of an officer who is under 
suspension the emoluments drawn by him on the first day 
of the allotment year in which he is placed under suspen 
sion , or if he is placed under suspension on the first day of 
the allotment year, the emoluments drawn by him immedia 
tely before that date shall be taken as emoluments ; 

(f ) family means the wife or husband , as the case may 
be, and children , step - children , legally adopted children , par 
ents , brothers or sister s as ordinarily reside with , and we 
dependent on , the officer ;

S .R . 317 - 3- 13 Surrender of an allotment and period of 
notice : 

( 1 ) An oficer may at any time surrender an allotment by 
giving intimation so as to rench the Director at least ten days 
before the date of vacation of the residence . The allotment 
of the residence shall be deemed to be cancelled with effect 
from the eleventh day after the day on which the letter is 
received by the Director, or the date specified in the letter, 
whichever is later . If he fails to give due notice , he shull 
be responsible for payment of licence fee for ten days or the 
number of days by which the notice given by him falls short 
of ten days, provided that the Director may accept it notico 
for a short period . 


S .R . 317-5 -18 Maintenance of residences : -- 

The Officer to whom a residence has been allotted shall 
maintain the residence and premiges in a clean condition 
to the satisfaction of the Director and the Municipal 
Corporation of Delhi. Such officer shall not grow any tree , 
shrubs of plants contrary to the instructions issued by the 
Director nor cut or lop off any existing tree shrubs in any 
garden , courtyard or compound attached to the residence, 
suve with the prior permission in writing of the Director. 
Trees, plantation or negetation , grown and rearing of poultry 
or cattle in contravention of these rules may be caused to be 
removed at the risk and the cost of the officer concerned . 


S . R . 317 -J - 19 Subletting and sharing of residences : 


( 1 ) No officer shall share the icsidence allotted to him 
or any of the out-housCA, garages and stables appurtenant 
thereto except with the employees of the Institute eligiblo 
for allotment of residences under these rules. The servants 
quarters , out-houses, garages and stables may be used only 
for thc bonafidc purposes including iesidence of tbc servants 
of the allottee or for such purposes ag inay be permitted by 
the Director 


( 2 ) No officer sball sublet the whole of his residenco 
provided that an officer piocoeding on leave may accommo 
date in the residence any other officer eligible to sharo ac 
commodation , as a carotaker , for the period specified in S . R . 
317 - J - 10 ( 2 ) , but not exceeding six months . 


( 2 ) An officer who surrendeis the residence under sub 
rulc ( 1 ) shall not be considered again for allotment of accom 
modation at the Institute for a period of one year from the 
date of such surrender . 
S .R . 317-5- 14 Change of residence : 

( 1 ) An officer to whoro a residence has been allotted under 
these nies may apply for a change to dnother residence of 
the same type or a residence of the type to which he is eiigi 
ble under S .R . 317- J - 5 whichever is lower, provided that not 
more than one change shall be allowed to any such officer in 
respect of one type of residence Allotted to the officer. 

( 2 ) All applications for change made in the form prcscrib 
ed by the Director and received upto the 19th day of a calen 
dar month shall be included in the waiting list in the subsc 
quent month . For the purpose of this rule , the officers whoso 
names are included in the waiting list, in an carlier month shall 
be senior on block to those whose names are included in the

( 3 ) Change shall be offered in order of seniority determined

officer s preferences, as far as possible .

S . R . 317 -J- 15 Change of residence in the event of death of a 
member of the family : 

Notwithstanding anything contained in S .R . 317 -J - 14 , an 
officer may be allowed a change of residence on the death 
of any member of his family if he applies for a change within 
three months of such occurrence , provided that the change may 
Ive given in the same type of residence and on the same floor 
as the residence already allotted to the Officer , 
S . R . 317 -J - 16 Mutual exchange of residence :- -- 

Officers to whom residences of the same type have been 
allotted tinder these rules may apply for permission for rutual 
exchange of their icsidonces . Permission of mutual exchange 
inay be granted if both the officers are icasonably expected to 
be on duty in Delhi and to reside in their mutually exchanged 
residences for at least six months from the date of approval 
of such exchange. 

S. R . 317- J- 17 Transfer to non -family stations : 


( 1 ) If an officer to whom a residence has been allotted 
unauthorisedly sublets the residence or charges rent from the

period in future as may be decided by the Director.

का० प्रा० 2126 – केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इसमे मंलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट पूरानस्वीय स्थल और अवशेष गष्ट्रीय महत्व का है ; 


प्रन. अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार , प्राचीन मस्मारक तथा पुरातन्वीय स्थल और अवशष अधिनियम , 1958 ( 1958 का 24 ) को धारा 4 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
द्वारा प्रक्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , उक्त पुगतस्वीय स्थन और अवशेष को राष्ट्रीय महत्व का घोषित करने के अपने प्राशय की सूचना देती है । 

इस अधिसूचना के जारी किए जाने के पश्चात् दो माम के भीतर उग्न पुरातत्वीय स्थल और अवशेप मे हितबब किसी व्यक्ति द्वारा किए गए किसी 
माक्षेप पर केन्द्रीय सरकार विचार करेगी ।

निर्देशित करती है ।

to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby imakes 
the following rules further to amend the Department of

1976 , namely :

Amendment Rules, 1977 .

tion in the Official Gazette .

3 . There are two points in dispute between the manage 
ment of the Moonidih Project and the United Coal Workers 
Union , Moonidih Branch . The first dispute relates to the 
proper classification of 9 workmen and the second dispute 
is in respect of payment of Project Allowance to all the 
workmen employed in the Moonidih Project. I propose to 
deal with these disputes in seriatim . 

4 . Bindeshwari Gope , Sukhdco Gope , Allu Mahato , 
Chhabinath Mahato , Kala Chand Gore , Jadu Gope , Fantush 
Mahato , Yudhisthir Karmakar, Bhim Gorai, Sudun Mahato , 
Lattu Mahato , Deo Muni Mahato and Jagdish Mahato claimed 
that they were being designated and shown its General Maz 
doors , Category - l, in the Moonidih Project and were being 
paid Category I wages even though they were actually per 
forming the duties of Tyndals " Heavy " category IV and were 
antitled to category IV wages . Their causo was takon up 
by the Secretary , United Cool Workers, Union , Moonidin 
Branch with the Project Officer, Moonidih Project on Febru 
ary 27 , 1973 by the letter Ext. W - 1 . The demand received 
no satisfaction and therefore , the Union served å strike 
notice Ext. M - 1 on the Project Officer on December 24, 1973 
of their intention to go on a strike from January 14 , 1974. The 
strike notice was accompanied by & Charter of 23 demands. 
Demand No, 4 reads thus : " Tyndal Mazdoors at present are 
being paid at the rate of Category I only being designated as 
General Mazdoors . All such workmen should be given the 
due designation of Tyndal Mazdoor and be placed in Cate 
yory V . The A . L . C . ( C ) called for the comments of the 
Project Officer on the charter of demands. Ext. M - 2 datert 
February 2 , 1974 contain the comments of the Moonidih 
Project on these 25 demands. With regard to demand No. 4 , 
the comments are as follows : " The statement is not correct . 
Porson performing the job of Tyndal has been placed in 
Category IV . The demand is vague. The Union is requested 
to submit specific names for the consideration of the manage 
ment." The Union was thereupon required to offer its com 
ments on the comments of the management. The Union s 
comments are contained in Ext. W -2 dated February 14 , 
1974 . The comments are " The specific cases of the concerned 
Tyndals (now kept in Category I as General Mazdoors ) was 
carlier forwarded to the management vide union s letter No. 
CWD /MND / 10 / 73, doled 27- 2 - 1973 . For ready reference 
the names are given below again ." Nine names were given 
and the names of Sudan Mahato , Lattu Mahato , Deo Muni 
Mahato and Jagdish Mabato were omitted because admittedly 
after the service of Ext. W - 1 and before the date of the 
comments , these four had been promoted by the Project 
Oficer as Tyndals , Cat . IV . The dispute this is confined 
to the remaining nine whose names are specified in item 
No. 1 , of the Schedule to the reference .

6 . Before entering into thc controversy , I would like to 
give certain facts which are not disputed , and are indeed , 
indisputable . Chapter VIII — Section - A , Voi, I of the Report 
of the Central Wage Board for the Coal Mining Industry 
deals with timc-rated categorics. In paragraph 15 of Chapter 
VIII - Section - A , the Board classified time-rated workmep into 
six categories. In paragraph 16 , the Board mentioned that 
their recommenations for ſitting existing workmon into the 
new six categoricg together with the nomenclatures and their 
job discriptions are contained in Appendix V to their report . 
Item No. 3 of Category I in Appendix V , relates to " Fitters" . 
It mentions that a fitter is a workman who accompanies a 
Fitter / Electrician / Carpenter etc . and carries tools or mate 
rials. Admittedly , these nine workmen have been classified 
as Category I workmen under this item . Item No . 22 of 
Catogory II, Appendix V rofers 10 " Fittor Helper" as a man 
who has gained some experience as & Fitter Mazdoor and 
has improved status by practice until he can do clemen 
tary fitting work . He may be a mechanical or electrical 
holper As tho caso may bo . Itom No . 5 of Category IV , 
Appendix V concerns Tyndals . A Tyndal is a man generally 
employed in moving engincering stores , drums of Oil and 
greuses. He is also responsible for crection , dismantaling 
of structures and installation and withdrawal of machinery . 
Item No. 1 of Category V , Appendix V , deals with a Tyndal 
Supervisor. He is a man experienced in handling materials 
anu in enginçering structures who is incharge of a gång of 
Tyndals (Heavy ) . The claim of those nine workmen is for 
classification under item No . V of Category IV . Para 19 
of Chapter VII — Section - A gives the wages of the workmen 
belonging to the six categories . The daily minimum wage 
of a Category I woskinan is Rs. 5 . There is an annual 
increment of 10 paise per day and the maximum daily wage 
rises to Rs. 6 in a span of 10 yeais. The daily minimum 
wage of a Category 1 workman is Rs. 6 . 90 There is an 
annual increment of 20 paise per day and the maximum 
daily wage rises to Rs. 8 . 90 in a duration of 10 years . The 
wages have been revised with effect from January 1, 1975 
by the Joint Bi- partite Wage Negotiating Committee . 
revised rate of Category I is Rs. 10 which rises to Rs. 12 

The 
in 10 years with an annual increment of 20 raise per day . 

The revised rate of Category IV workman is Rs. 12 . 15 
which rises to Rs. 16 .85 in 10 ycars with an gnnual incro 
Ment of 41 paise per day.

Memorandum say , that the project allowance is to be given 
only for lui ge- scale projects and not for the construction of 
individual buildings. It will not be admissible oncept in such 
projects which have been declaied by specinl orders of Go 
vernment its qualifying for grant of the same. Generally , 
the allowance will be sanctioned only if the execution of the 
project involves the establishment of a large construction or 
ganisation and the construction is sprcad over a number of 
years . The allowance is intended primarily to compensate 
the staff for lack of amenities such an , housing schools, 
markets , diepensaries etc. Where reasonable amenities already 
exist, there would be no justification for the grant of the 
allowance . The allowanco will not be admissible to staff re 
cruited locally on ad-hoc scales of pay such as daily -rutod or 
casual labour and staff paid from contingency. The allowance 
will be admissible only to guch staff as are employed on 
the project and resido within tho project area of in a 
nearby locality . The allwance may also be granted to such 
Central Government staff of other departments as have 
their Officers located in the project area for the work of the 
project, provided that they reside within the project area 
or in a nearly locality . The term " project area " will be the 
aroa defined as such by Government in respect of each pro 
fect for which the project allowance is sanctionod . The pro 
ject allowance shall be subject to the following ceilings :

is a letter from the Administrative Officer to the Personnel 
Officer cnquiring whether the personnel who were previously 
recruited in a project but were subsequently transferred to a

which says that such of the employees who had been trans

the project allowance was given only to officers and monthly 
rated staff. Ex. M - 17 are the Minutes of the 55th Mecting

indicate that the allowance was given only to officers

which directs that the project allowance was being granted

dated July 10 , 1964 maintaining the status que with regard

paid staff employed in 31 projects, including the Moonidih 
Project, specified at serial no . 18 . That the Office Memo 
Tundum issued by the Ministry of Finance was acted upon 
by the N . C . D . C . in respect of phased reduction and with 
drawal of the allowance is also supported by various docu 
ments . Ext. M - 18 ( the note prepared for the 55th Meeting ) 
proposed the continuance of the reduced rato in the Korba 
Project and Ext. M - 17 is the Minutes of the Meeting confirm 
ing the proposal. Ext. M - 8 containg the scheme for the 
progressive withdrawal of the project allowance . The scheme 
provides for a 50 per cent reduction in the project allowanco 
once an employee was given a house with water, light and 
internal sanitation . This , in effect, meant that once a pukka 
house was allotted with water, light and internal sanitation , 
thee was d minimum reduction of 50 per cent of the project 
allowance in respect of the individual to whom the house 
was allotted while other employees who may not have been 
allotted houses would continue to be entitled to the full 
project allowance till such time as they were allotted house 
with the necessary facilitics of water, light and Internal sani 
tation . The scheme further provided that a further 25 per 
cent ieduction would be effected on completion of hospitals , 
schools and markets . This meant that once these amenities 
were provided, those already given houses will have a further 
reduction of 25 per cent in the project allowance , whilo 
thosc who may not have been allotted houses would , in any 
case , have to accept a reduction of 25 per cent. The schemo 
further provides that once filtered water supply , and Workers 
Institutes and hospitals in terms of the rules of the Coal 
Miney Welfare Commissioner are provided , the project 
allowance will be fully withdrawn and would be applicable 
to all employees even if some of the employees have got 
occupied Corporation quarters. Ext. M -6 Office Order No. 
13, 1964 defines " houso " , " hospital" , " school" and " Altered 
water " . It further mentions that the reduction of 25 per 
cent with regard to filtered water, Workers Instituto and 
hospital, will comprisc 15 per cent for the provision of the 
amenity of filtered water supply and 10 per cent for Worker , 
Institute and hospital, but no cut of 10 per cent will be made 
where only one of these two amenities, namely , Workers 
Institute or hospital is provided . Ext. M -11 dated December 
18 , 1964 shows that where house and filtered water were 
supplied the project allowance was reduced by 65 per cent. 
Ext. M - 16 dated June 10 , 1966 also shows the game facto . 
Ext. M - 12 is not relevant as it deals with amenities or lack 
of them not in project areas but in regular collieries .

The Government will fix the quantum of the allowance at 
the time of sanctioning it and also each time it is revised , 
The continuance of tho allowance would not be justified for 
the rogular staff of the project when the necessary amenities 
have been provided in the project area . Hence, the allowance 
sanctioned originally for such staff shall be reduced in stages 
as the necessary amenities are provided , until it is withdrawn 
finally . As an exception , in the case of temporary construc 
tion staff who have not been absorbed finally in the project 
on a regular basis , the allowance may be continued on a 
reduced sculo until such staff are finally discharged , so long 
as construction of any magniture goes on . The principles 
mentioned above which apply to projects run by Govern 
ment departmentally may also be communicated to Com 
panies / Corporation etc. under the Ministry of Commerce 
and Industry etc. for information and guidance .

in 1975- 76 . He further deposed that the projcct incurred

jects rcquirc a heavy investinent, which sturts yielding profits

Besides , if thc project 18 prepared to incur loss and yet pay 
tho allowance to some categories , it could as well pay to 
other categories also , or abrogate the allowance for all and 
not retain it for some and deny it to olhery. Again , the 
figures worked out include the figurce for officers and 
monthly - rated staff to whom the allowance is already being 
given and these figures should have been excluded to find 
out the additional expenditure involved if the allowance is 
paid to those who are at present excluded. Another ground 
taken is that the daily and piece - rated workmen are entitled 
to the benefits of C . M . P . F . Scheme but the officers and 
monthly -rated staff are dcbáited from joining that scheme 
and thus if thoy lose in onc matter, they gain in another . 
This ground is correct in part only , Clause ( g ) of para 2 
of the C . M . P . F . Scheme excludes the old employces of the 
N . C . D . C . from the benefits of the Scheme. The daily and 
piece raters are , however , not excluded because they are 
governed by Coal Wage Board pay scales . They contribute 
at tho rate of 8 per cent of their total wage- packet : Basic , 
D . A ., V . D . A ., U . G . A ., O . T . etc - and the N . C .D . Ç . also 
makes a matching contribution . The N .C . D . C . has, however, 
its own separate P . F . Scheme for its officers and monthly . 
raters who contribute 817 per cent of their basic wages 
only and the N . C . D . C . makes a matching contribution of 
its own . This is a very minor difference which can bo 
ignored . Another ground taken is that whereas daily -ratern 
and piccc -raters have to pay a rent of Rs. 2 per month only 
for their quarters, an officer and a monthly - rater has to pay 
10 per cent of his salary or the standard rent whichever is less 
and here also the daily and plece raters stand to gain . This 
ground has no substance whatsoever. The project allowance 
is given , inter alia , for the absence of housing facilities . 
Officers and monthly - raters , like daily - raters and picco- rater 
have not to pay any house rent, because there are no houses 
in the project area and if and when a house is provided , 
hoth have to pay , and the former gets a reduced allowance .

AWARD 
The Central Government, in exercise of the powers con 
ferred by clause (d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) of section 10 of the 
Industrial Disputes Act referred the following dispute for 
adjudication to the Contral Government Indus 
trial Tribunal No. 2 , Dhanbad by Order No . L - 2012 / 179 / 
74 -LRII /DIA, dated , the 19th April, 1975, namely , 


" 1 . Whether the management of Chagnala Colliery of 

Messrs. Indian Jron and Stcel Company Limited , 
Post Office Patherdih , District Dhanbad, are justified 
in not making payment of overtime wages at 2 - 1 / 2 
times normal rato of wages or 1- 1 / 2 times normal 
rato of wages plus a compensatory day of rest for 
work done on Sunday , tho common weekly rest 
day ? If not, to what relief are the workmen en 
titled to ? 


24. My award on the first dispute is that Bindeswarl Gope , 
Sukhdoo Gope , Aklu Mahato , Chhabinath Mahato , Kalachand 
Gope , Jadu Gope, Funtush Mahato , Yudhistir Karmakar 
and Bhim Gorain are not Tyndal Mistrios and are thus not 
entitled to wages of a Category IV workman . However, 
Bindeswari Gope , Sukhdco Gope, Chhabinath Mahato and 
Aklu Mahato are Fitter -helpers , Category If and not Fittors , 
Category J. Bindeswari Gope, Sukhdeo Gope and Chhabi 
nath Mahato have been accepted by the management as 
Category II workmen , Aklu Mahato is not being treated 
as Category II workman . He will be so trcated and raid 
the wages of Category II from January 1 , 1974 since which 
date he is working as a Fitter-helper. My Award on the 
socond dispute is that the project allowance will not bo paid 
to such of the daily raters and piece-raters as are original 
inhabitants of villages within a radius of 5 miles of the 
project area . It will, however , be paid to such daily - raters 
and piece - raters as are not original inhabitants of villages 
within the radius of 5 miles of the project area . The manage 
ment will work out the two categories to fix the persons to 
whom it will be paid and to whom it will not be paid . In 
order to ensure that the management is not saddled with a 
heavy financial commitment in respect of the previous years, 
I direct that the award in respect of the project allowance 
shall be enforceable from February 3, 1975 when the refe 
rence was made . 


2. Whether the management of Chaşnala Colliery of 

Messrs . Indian Iron and Steel Company Limited , 
Post Office Patherdih , District Dhanbad , are Justi 
fied in transferring Sarva Shri (i) D . D . Singh , Dos 
patch Clerk , ( ii ) K . K . Chatterjee , Register Keeper , 
(iii) Mahabir Mahato , Weigh Bridge Clerk and (iv ) 
R K . Routh , Watchman , from 14 - 10 - 1974 ? If not 
to what relief are the workmen ontitled ?" 


2 . While the reference was pending in Tribunal No , 2 , 
the parties filed a settlement in respect of Issuo No. 2 on 
September 16 , 1976 and that settlement in Annexure A , 


3 . Tho reference was received on transfer from Tribunal 
No. 2 in this Tribunal on March 22 , 1977 vide Government 
on India , Ministry of Labour, Order No . S -11025 ( 1 ) /77 -(i) 
Div ( B ) dated February 22 , 1977 . Here also the parties filed 
a second settlement with regard to Tssue No. 1 and that 
settlement in Annexure B .

EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM 
It has become necessary to give retrospective effect to 
thc exemption in this case, as the request from the employer 
for the grant of cxemption was received late . However , it is 
certificd that the factory is eligible for exemption . It is also 
certified that the grant of exemption with retrospective effect 
will not affect the interest of anybody udveisely .

It has become nccessary to give retrospective effect to the 
excmption in this case , as the recommendation of the Director 
General, Employces State Insurance Corporation for the grant 
of exemption was recoived late . However, it is certified that 
the conditions under which the rogular employees of the 
factory were initially granted exemption still persist and they 
are cligible for exemption . It is also certified that the grant 
of exemption with retrospective effect will not affect the in 
terest of anybody adversely .

exemption in this case , 29 the request of the employer for 
grant of exemplion was received late. However, it is certified 
that the factory is eligible for exemption , it is also certified 
that the grant of exemption with retrospective effect will not 
affect the interest of anybody adversely .

का० आ० 2 169 . - ममे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट प्रौद्योगिक 
विवाद , पीठामीन अधिकारी , केन्द्रीय मरकार औद्योगिक प्रधिकरण, दिल्ली 
के समक्ष लम्बिन है । 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रशासनिक कारणो मे उक्त विवादो को केन्द्रीय 
मरकार औद्योगिक अधिकरण, नई दिल्ली को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए हस्तात 
रित करना वांछनीय समझती है । 

प्रत., अम, फेन्द्रीय मरकार प्रौद्योगिक वियाद अधिनियम , 19.17 
( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 33व को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियो 
का प्रयोग करते हए , उक्त विवाद में सम्बद्ध कार्यवाहियो को 
पीठामीन अधिकारी केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण , दिल्ली मे 
वापस लेती है और उन्हें उक्क अधिनियम की धारा 7 क के अधीन 
गठिन वन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिफ अधिकरण , नई दिल्ली का हम्मातरित 
करती है और निदेश देती है कि उक्त केन्द्रीय सरकार औद्यागिक अधिकरण , 
नई दिल्ली उक्त कार्यवाहियां उसी प्रक्रम से करेगा जिस पर वे उमे हस्तात 
रित की गई है और विधि के अनमार उनका निपटान करेगा ।

s . o . 2169.-.- Whoroas the industrial disputes specified 
in the Schedule hereto annexed are pending before the Presiding 
Officer, Central Government Industrial Tribunal, Delhi: 

And whereas for administrative roasons , the Central 
Government considors it desirable to transfor the said disputos 
for adjudication to the Central Government Industrial Tribunal, 
New Delhi ;

20 . C . G .I. D . No . 59 of 1975 


Now therefore , in exercise of the power s conferred 
by sub - section ( 1 ) of section 33B of thc Industrial Disputes Act, 
1947 ( 14 of 1947 ), the Central Government hereby withdraws 
the proceedings in relation to the said dispute from the Presi 
ding Officer, Central Govornment Industrial Tribunal, Delhi 
and transfers the samo to the Central Government Industrial 
Tribunal, New Delhi constituted undor section 7A of the said 
Act and directs that the said Central Governincnt Industrial 
Tribunal , New Delhi, shall proceed with the said proceedings 
from the stage at which they are transferred to it and dispose 
of the same according to law .

का० प्रा० 2 170 . ---केन्द्रीय सरकार, लौह अयस्क खान श्रम कल्याण 
उपकर नियम , 1963 के नियम 31 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करसे 
हुए , मध्य प्रवेश राज्य में लौह अयस्क खान श्रम कल्याण संगठन के कल्याण 
मायुक्त को लौह अयस्क खान उपकर प्रायुत विनिर्दिष्ट करती है जो लोह 
अयस्क खान श्रम कल्याण उपकर अधिनियम , 1961 ( 1981 की 58 ) 
की धारा 2 के खंड ( ख ) के अधीन उद्ग हीत और एक प्रगतूबर, 1974 
के पूर्व शोध्य हुए उत्पाद शुल्क के निर्धारण और संग्रहण के लिए अपनी 
अधिकारिता के क्षेत्र के भीतर अनन्यता उत्त रादायी होगा । 

[ फा० सं० एम० 230 1 3/ 2/ 74- एम० 4 ]

का० प्रा० 2171 .---निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) 
अधिनियम , 1963 ( 1963 का 22 ) की धारा 6 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियो 
का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार की यह राय है कि भारत के निर्यात 
व्यापार के विकास के लिए ऐसा करना आवश्यक मथा समीमीन है कि 
प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरें निर्यात से पूर्व क्वालिटी नियंत्रण एवं निरीक्षण के अधीन 
हो , 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त प्रयोजन के लिए नीचे विनिर्दिष्ट प्रस्ताव 
बनाए है और उन्हे निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1964 के नियम II के उपनियम ( 2 ) द्वारा अपेक्षित के अनुसार निर्यान 
निरीक्षण परिषद् को भेज दिया है । 
__ अतः अब, उक्त उपनियम के अनुसरण में केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त प्रस्तावों 
को उन लोगों की जानकारी के लिए जिनकी उनसे प्रभावित होने की 
सम्भावना है , प्रकाशित करती है ।

2. यह सूचना दी जाती है कि यदि कोई व्यक्ति उपस प्रस्तावो के 
बारे में कोई प्राक्षेप या सुप्ताय देना चाहे तो वह उन्हें इस आदेश के 
भारत के राजपत्र में प्रकाशन की तारीख से पैतालीम दिनो के भीतर 
निर्यात निरीक्षण परिषद्, थर्ड ट्रेड सेन्टर , 11/ 1-बी , एजरा स्ट्रीट ( 8वीं 
मंजिल ) कलकत्ता - 700001 को भेज सकेगा । 

प्रस्ताव 
( 1 ) यह अधिसूचित करना कि औद्योगिक जजीरें निर्यात से पूर्व 
स्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण के अधीन होंगी ; 

( 2 ) इम भावेश के उपाबन्ध - 1 में दिए गए प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरों का 
निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1976 के प्रारूप के 
अनुसार निरीक्षण के प्रकार का निरीक्षण के उम प्रकार के रूप में 
विनिविष्ट करना जो ऐसी प्रौद्योगिफः जंजीरो पर निर्यात में पूर्व लागू 
होगा ; 

( 3) ( क ) विदेणी कसा एवं निर्यात कर्ता के मध्य निर्यात मंविदा 
में पणित विनिर्देशों का मान्यता देना ; 

( ख ) स्तम्भ ( क ) में निर्दिष्ट किमी भी विनिर्देश की अनुपस्थिति 
में प्रौद्योगिफ जंजीरों के लिए भारतीय मानक संस्थान या विदेश के 
राष्ट्रीय मानको द्वारा जारी किए गए विनिर्देशों को मानक विनिर्देशों के 
रूप में मान्यता देना ; 

( 4) अन्तर्राष्ट्रीय व्यापार के दौरान ऐसी प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरों के 
निर्यात को तब तक प्रतिषिद्ध करना जब तक कि उसके साथ निर्यात 
( क्वालिटी नियन्त्रण और निरीक्षण ) अधिनियम , 1963 ( 1963 का 
22 ) की धारा 7 के अधीन केन्द्रीय मरकार द्वारा स्थापित अभिकरणों 
में से किसी एक द्वारा जारी किया इस प्राशय का प्रमाण-पत्र न हो कि 
प्रौद्योगिक जजीरें क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण से सम्बन्धिप्त पतों 
को पूरा करती है तथा निर्यात -योग्य है ।

होगी ।

जंजीर या लीफ जंजीर अभिप्रेस हैं ।

3. क्वालिटी नियन्त्रण : ( 1 ) निर्यात के लिए प्राशयित प्रौद्योगिक 
जंजीरों की फ्यालिटी इससे उपाषर अनुसूची में दिए गए नियंत्रण की 
परखो के साथ विनिर्माण के विभिन्न स्तरों पर निम्नलिखिम नियन्त्रणों का 
प्रयोग करके सुनिश्चित की जाएगी, अर्थात् : 
( i ) खरीदी गई सामग्री तथा संघटक नियन्त्रण : 
( क ) प्रयुक्त किए जाने वाले घटकों तथा सामग्री के गुण धर्मों को 

ममाविष्ट करते हुए विनिर्माता द्वारा क्रय विनिर्देश अभिकथित 
किए जाएंगे और उसके पास पाने वाले लाटो की अनुरूपता सुनिश्चित 

करने के लिए निरीक्षण या परख के पर्याप्त साधन होंगे । 
( ख ) स्वीकृत परेपणो के साथ या तो ऋय विनिर्देशों की अपेक्षाओं 

की पुष्टि करते हुए प्रदाय -कर्ता का परीक्षण या निरीक्षण प्रमाण-पत्र 
होगा, उस दशा में यदा-कदा होने वाली जांच (मर्थात् माल में हर 
तीन महीनों में एक बार उसी माल के उसी प्रदाय-फर्ता के लिए ) 
विशिष्ट प्रवाय-कर्ता के लिए क्रेता द्वारा उक्त परख या निरीक्षण 
प्रमाण-पत्रों की शुद्धता सत्यापित करने के लिए की आएगी या क्रय 
किए गए माल या घटकों का या तो कारखाने की प्रयोगशाला में 
या किसी अन्य प्रयोगशाला या परीक्षण गृह में नियमित रूप से 

निरीक्षण या परख की जाएगी । 
( ग ) फिए जाने वाले निरीक्षण या परण के लिए नमूना लेना लेखबर 

किए गए अन्वेषण पर आधारित होगा । 
( घ ) निरीक्षण या परस्त्र किए जाने के पश्चात् , स्वीकृत तथा अस्वीकृत 

माल या घटकों के पृथक्करण में तथा अस्वीकृत माल या 

घटकों के निपटान में व्यवस्थित पद्धतियां अपनाई जाएंगी । 
( ) ऊपर निर्विष्ट नियंत्रणों के सम्बन्ध में विनिर्माता द्वारा पर्याप्त 

अभिलेख व्यवस्थित तथा नियमित रूप से रखे जाएंगे । 
( ii ) प्रक्रम नियंत्रण 
( क ) विनिर्माण की विभिन्न प्रक्रमों के लिए विनिर्माता द्वारा विस्तृत 

प्रक्रम विनिर्देश अधिकथित किए जाएंगे । 
ब ) प्रक्रिया विनिर्देशो में अधिकथित प्रक्रियाभो के नियंत्रण के लिए 

उपस्कर , उपकरण सुविधाएं पर्याप्त होंगी । 


( ग ) विनिर्माण की प्रक्रिया के दौरान प्रयुक्त नियंत्रणों के मत्यापन 

की सभावनाओं को सुनिश्चित करने के लिये विनिर्माता द्वारा 
पर्याप्त अभिलेख रखे जायेंगे । 


(iii ) उत्पाव नियंत्रण : 
( क ) विनिर्माता के पास अधिनियम की धारा 6 के अधीन मान्य

स्वयं की अपनी परख सुविधाये होंगी या अन्य स्थान पर 

विद्यमान ऐमी परख सुविधाओं तक उसकी पहुंच होगी ।

किये गये अन्वेषण पर प्राधारित होगा । 
ग ) की गई परखों के सम्बन्ध में पर्याप्त प्रभिलेख विनिर्माता द्वारा 

व्यवस्थित एवं नियमित रूप से रखे जायेंगे । 


( iv ) परिरक्षण नियंत्रण :

रखने के लिये विनिर्माता विस्तृत विनिर्देश बनाएगा ।

तरह से परिरक्षित किया जायेगा ।

( v ) मौसम मम्बन्धी नियंत्रण : 

( क ) निर्यातकर्ता की वणा में , यदि वह स्वयं ही विनिर्माता है, तो , 

उमक यह ममाधान कर लेने पर कि विनिर्माण की प्रक्रिया 
उत्पावम नया निरीक्षण में प्रयुक्त मापकों एवं उपकरणों की कालिक 

के दौरान उमने इस पर लागू होने वाले मानक विनिर्देशों के 
जांच या अंशशोधन किया जायगा और विनिर्माता द्वारा अभिलेख वृनका 

अनुमार उत्पाद का पिनिर्माण करने के लिये इस सम्बन्ध में 
के रूप में रम्भे जाएंगे । 

परिषद द्वारा जारी किये गये अनुदेशों, यदि कोई हों , तथा 

नियम 3 में दिये गये पर्याप्त मवालिटी नियंत्रणों का प्रयोग 
( vi ) पैकिंग नियमण : 

गया है । 
निर्यात पंकजों के लिये विनिर्माता ब्यौरेवार पैकिंग विनिर्देश अमायेगा 
तथा उसका सससी से पालन करेगा । 

( ब ) निर्यात कर्ता फी वणा में , यदि वह स्वय विनिर्माता नही है 

तो , उमके यह ममाधान कर लेने पर कि उसने विनिर्माण की 
( 2 ) निरीक्षण---निर्मान के लिये आशयित प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरों का 

प्रक्रिया के दौरान, इम पर लागू मानक विनिर्देशों के अनुसार 
निरीक्षण यह देखने के विचार से किया जायेगा कि क्या ऊपर वर्णित 

उत्पाद का विनिर्माण करने के लिये इस सम्बन्ध में परिषद 
नियंत्रणों का मुसंगत स्तरों पर ममाधान रूप से प्रयोग किया गया है 

मारा जारी किये गये विनिर्देशों, यदि कोई हों तथा नियम 3 में दिये 
पौर प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरे इस पर लागू मानक विनिर्देशों के अनुरूप है । 

गये पर्याप्त मवालिटी नियंत्रणो का प्रयोग किया है तो वह निरीक्षण 

करने के तीन दिनों के भीतर यह पोषणा करते हुए प्रमाण 
___ 4. निरीक्षण की प्रक्रिया :--- ( 1 ) निर्यातकर्ता किसी भी अभिकरण 

पन्न जारी करेगा कि प्रायोगिक जंजीर का परेषण निर्यात योग्य 
को लिखित रूप में भूचना देगा और ऐमी सूचना के साथ एक घोषणा 
पन्न देगा कि प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरों का परेषण नियम 3 में निर्दिष्ट नियंत्रणों 
के अनुमार क्वालिटी नियंत्रण परिमापों का प्रयोग करके निर्मित किया 

परन्तु जहां अभिकरण को इस प्रकार का समाधान नहीं 
गया है या किया जा रहा है और परेषण उक्त प्रयोजन के लिये मान्यता 

हुआ है कहा वह उक्त तीन दिनों की अवधि के भीतर ऐसा 
प्राप्त विनिर्देशों की अपेक्षाओं के अनुरूप है । निर्यातकर्ता उसी समय ऐमी 

प्रमाण पत्र देने से इंकार कर देगा नथा ऐसे इंकार की सूचना 
सूचना की एक प्रतिलिपि परिषद् के निकटतम कार्यालय को भेजेगा । 

उसके लिये कारणों सहित निर्यातकर्ता को देगा । 
परिषद के पते इस प्रकार हैं :- - 


मुख्य कार्यालयः 
निर्यात निरीक्षण परिषद , 
14/ 1- बी , एजरा स्ट्रीट ( 8वीं मंजिल )

5. मान्य चिन्ह का चिपकाना और उसकी प्रक्रिया :- - भारतीय मानफ 
मस्थान ( प्रमाणीकरण चिन्ह ) अधिनियम , 1952 ( 1952 का 36 ) , 
भारतीय मानक संस्थान ( प्रमाणीकरण चिन्ह ) नियम, 1955 तथा भारतीय 
मानक संस्थान ( प्रमाणीकरण चिन्ह ) विनियम , 1955 के उपबन्ध, जहां 
तक हो , प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरों पर निर्यात से पूर्व माम्य चिन्ह या सील के 
चिपकाने की प्रक्रिया के सम्बन्ध में लागू होंगे तथा इस प्रकार चिन्हित 
प्रौद्योगिक जजीरें नियम 4 के अन्तर्गत किसी भी निरीक्षण के अधीन नही 
होंगी । 


___ G. निरीक्षण का स्थान :- - इन नियमों के अधीन प्रत्येक निरीक्षण विनि 
माता या निर्यातकर्ता के परिसर पर पोत लवान की बन्दरगाह पर किया 
जायेगा ।

7. निरीक्षण फीस :- - नियम 4 के अन्तर्गत प्रौद्योगिक जंजीरों के 
ऐसे प्रत्येक परेषण के लिये पर्यंत निःशुल्क मूल्य के प्रत्येक एक सौ रुपये 
पर पचास पैसे की दर से पोत निरीक्षण फीस के रूप में निर्यातकर्ता द्वारा 
भभिकरण को दी जायेगी । यह फीस कम से कम एक सौ रुपये होगी ।

लिये केन्द्रीय सरकार द्वारा नियुक्त कम से कम तीन लेकिन अधिकतम सात 
व्यक्तियों के विशेषज्ञो के पनल को अपील कर सकेगा ।

MINISTRY OF COMMERCE 

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 25th June, 1977 
8. 0 . 2171 . -- Whereas the Central Government is of opinion 
that it is necessary and expedient so to do for the development 
of the export trade of India that in exercise of the powers 
conferred by section 6 of the Export (Quality Control and 
Inspection ) Act, 1963 ( 22 of 1963) INDUSTRIAL CHAINS 
should be subject to quality control and inspection prior to 
export; 

And whereas the Central Government has formulated the 
proposals specified below for the said purpose and has for 


2 , Notice is hereby given that any person desiring to for 
ward any objections or suggestions with respect to the said 
proposals may forward the same within forty - five days of the 
date of publication of this order in the Gazette of India to 
the Export Inspection Council, " World Trade Centre " , 14 / 1B , 
Ezra Street (7th floor ), Calcutta - 700001 .

7 . Inspection Fee A fee at the rate of FIFTY paise for 
every hundred rupees of F . O . B . Value subject to a minimum 
of rupees ONE HUNDRED for each such consignment of 
Industrial chains shall be paid by the exporter to the agency 
as inspection fee under rule 4 . 


8 . Appeal. - ( 1 ) Any person aggrieved by the Icfusal of the 
agency to issue a certificatc under sub - rule ( 4 ) of rule 4 , may , 
within ten days of the receipt of the communication of such 
refusal by him , prefer an appeal to a panel of cxporter con 
sisting of not less than three but not more than seven per 
sons, appointed for the purpose by the Central Government. 

(2 ) At least two thirds of the total membership of the 
panel of experts shall consist of non -officials . 

(3 ) The quorum for the panel shall be threo . 
(4 ) The uppeal shall be disposed of within 15 days of 

its receipt.

कामा० 2172 -- ~ केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि निर्यात ( क्वालिटी 
नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) अधिनियम , 1963( 1963 का 22 ) की धारा 

द्वारा प्रदत शक्तियो का प्रयोग करते हुए, भारत के निर्याप्त व्यापार के 
विकास के लिए ऐसा करना प्रावश्यक और समीचीन है कि बीघडीमर 
निर्यान से पूर्व क्यालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण के अधीन हो ; 

और , केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त प्रयोजन के लिए नीचे विनिर्दिष्ट प्रस्ताव 
बनाए है तथा उन्हे निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंक्षण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1964 के नियम 11 के उप -नियम ( 2 ) की अपेक्षानुसार निर्यात निरीक्षण 
परिपद को भेज दिया है , 

प्रतः, अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त उप -नियम के अनुमरण में उक्त 
प्रस्तावों को उन लोगो की जानकारी के लिए प्रकाशित करती है जिनके 
उनमे प्रभावित होने की संभावना है । 

मुचना दी जाती है कि उक्त प्रस्तावों के बारे में कोई प्राक्षेप या 
मुझाव देने की बाछा रखने वाला कोई व्यक्ति उन्हें इस आदेश के राजपत्र 
में प्रकाशन की तारीख मे 45 दिनो के भीतर भारतीय निर्यात निरीक्षण 
परिषद् , बर्ड ट्रेड मेन्टर , 14/- यो , एजरा स्ट्रीट ( पाठवी मंजिल ) , 
कलकत्ता- 700001 को भेज सकता है । 


कि ऐसे बीचडीमर का परेवण कालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण संबंधी 
शर्तों को पूरा करता है और निर्यात योग्य है । 

2. इस प्राधेश की कोई भी बात भाषी क्रेताओं को भूमि , जल या 
वायु मार्ग द्वारा बीघडीभर के उन नमूनों के निर्यात पर लागू नहीं होगी

परन्तु जहाँ अभिकरण का इस प्रकार समाधान नहीं होता है बड़ा

( 6 ) अभिकरण, यदि अनिवार्य पूर्व-गोन दिदान योजना का समाधान 
करने के लिए यह प्रापश्यफ समझता है तो , निरीक्षन परेषण का उसके 
यास्तविक पोत- मदान से पूर्व भंडारीकरण के किसी स्थान पर , भिवहन 
में या जहाज बाट पर ऐसा पर्यवेक्षण करेगा । 


विनिर्देशो के अनुरूप है परीक्षण करने के माधन देगा और निर्यात -कर्ता उमी 
समय ऐसी सूचना की एक प्रति निरीक्षण के लिा निर्यात निरीक्षण परिषद् 
के निकटतम कार्यालय को देगा । 

( 2 ) उप -नियम ( 1 ) के अन्तर्गत प्रत्येक प्रार्थना पत्र विनिर्माता के 
परिसर से भेजे जाने के निर्धारित ममय से कम से कम मात दिन पहले 
अभिकरण के कार्यालय में पहुंभेगी । 

( 3 ) उप -नियम ( 2 ) मे निदिष्ट प्रार्थना -पन्न के प्राप्त होने पर 
अभिकरण बीचडीमर के परेषण का निरीक्षण, इसके लिए 
निर्यात निरीक्षण परिषद् द्वारा समय-समय पर जारी किए गए प्रदेशों के 
अनुसार यह देखने के विचार से करेगा कि क्या वह मान्य विनिर्देशो की 
प्रपेक्षत्रों के अनुरूप है । 

( 4) निर्यातकर्ता अभिकरण को ऐमा निरीक्षण करने के लिए मभी 
आवश्यक सुविधाएं देगा । 

( 5 ) निरीक्षण की ममाप्ति के पश्चात् यदि अभिकरण का समाधान 
हो जाए कि निर्यात किए जाने वाले वीचडीमर का परेषण नियम 3 में 
निविष्ट मान्य विनिर्देशो की अपेक्षानो के अनुरूप है तो अभिकरण प्रार्थना 
पन के प्राप्त होने के सात दिन के भीतर वह घोषणा करते हुए प्रमाण -पन्न 
दे देगा कि बीचडीमर का परेषण क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण में 
सम्बन्धित शर्तों को संतुष्ट करता है और निर्यात -योग्य है ।
